- **x442-register-cross-repo-graph**: Use when a project needs read-only access to another repo's code graph — e.g. a frontend session resolving a backend symbol, or any "reference another project's source" / cross-repo / monorepo-sibling lookup — so agents query the sibling graph instead of grepping across folders and burning tokens. Declare the sibling repos in a per-project `.graph-repos.json` (a user → repo → subdirectory cascade, like AGENTS.md), then sync — it registers them with code-review-graph, merges a per-project graphify graph, and rewrites the routing block in AGENTS.md. - **x442-register-delegate-agents**: Use when the set of agents available for delegation changes — a new local LLM appears in LM Studio or Ollama, a new CLI alias or custom config dir is set up, a project must be restricted to a subset of them, or you want to see which agents a repo actually permits and why. - **x442-repair-graph-hooks**: Use when the code knowledge graph or its hooks misbehave, drift out of sync, or after verify-graph-hooks reports a [FAIL]/[warn] — anything about graph tools "not working", stale/empty graph results, duplicate graph rebuilds, or a broken code-review-graph/graphify install. - **x442-setup-delegate-agent**: Use when a repo should be able to hand mechanical work to a cheaper agent running as a separate CLI process — "set up delegation here", "wire up the local LLM for this project", or any mention of dispatching bulk work to a sub-agent. - **x442-setup-graph-hooks**: Use immediately after initial-project on a new repo, or whenever the user mentions a code knowledge graph, graph hooks, code-review-graph, graphify, smart-grep, or blast-radius/impact analysis — anything about agents querying a graph instead of grepping/re-reading files. Wires self-updating, tool-generic graph hooks (Claude Code, Gemini CLI, GitHub Copilot, plus a git post-commit refresh) and a routing block in AGENTS.md, then verifies they fire. - **x442-setup-handoff**: Use immediately after initial-project, or whenever the user wants multi-agent / cross-session / cross-repo work coordination — a handoff board, "claim before you work / release when you stop" leases, tracking who acts next, or verifying "done" against live code. Idempotent and safe on any repo. Chains before run-handoff. ([xyzxyz442/x442-skills](https://github.com/xyzxyz442/x442-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/setup-handoff))
- **x442-setup-project-tooling**: Use after initial-project, or whenever setting up project dev tooling — commit conventions (commitlint + husky), staged-file lint/format (lint-staged), a VS Code workspace, or release automation (release-it), including migrating off commitizen and standard-version. Fully supports Python, Node/TypeScript (strict) and Unity projects; other languages get the common base to customize. ([xyzxyz442/x442-skills](https://github.com/xyzxyz442/x442-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/setup-project-tooling))
- **x442-setup-secret-guard**: Use when credential values must not reach an agent transcript — "stop Claude reading my .env", "redact secrets", "why did it print my API key", or any request to guard credential files across projects. ([xyzxyz442/x442-skills](https://github.com/xyzxyz442/x442-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/setup-secret-guard))
